## Independent verification and implications

The coordinator independently authenticated all 64 currency appendix blob pins
and all 33 quest source/helper blob pins against their specified Git trees.
All three relative links in each document resolve in its published bundle tree;
each exact commit passes `git diff --check` and has only its assigned path.
Critical behavior claims were compared with maintained bodies, including
`SUB_MONEY`, currency completion publication/ACK ordering, native quest dispatch,
cost projection, disappearance refusal and original QP02 terms. This is source
review. No build, database, server, migration, crash or gameplay test was run for
these document-only bundles. Worker-reported catalog checks remain reported
static evidence, not coordinator-executed native acceptance.

The currency map is useful input to a later DB-authoritative facade: preparation,
submission, durable commit, live projection, ACK and cold restore have different
owners. `SUB_MONEY` returns submission admission for its asynchronous PC route;
it does not establish payment completion. Existing pure preparation and optional
R2 extraction must be reused rather than duplicated. Compound coin/item work
retains its existing atomic writer and physical publication owner. No production
facade or RAM authority cut is approved by this design review.

The quest blueprint removes concrete fixture ambiguity. QP02's unpaid three-hide
branch is the first genuine target. A paid four-hide target still needs an
authentic reachable preselection chronology: ordinary sequential GIVE can finish
a lower-count branch first. Funding, if supplied by an eventual supported route,
is its own operation, and the quest fee is charged to the native recipient's
mapped wallet. Current active quest dispatch also refuses numeric offerings;
the specified conditional funding example is not a presently executable setup.
Do not replace these dependencies with seeded stock/cash or rewritten Q terms.

For QP03, maintained `item_movement_transaction.c:7694` refuses successful
D-required publication. An economic receipt may already be committed. A stable
held retry may be compared with another stable held cut; a precommit-to-committed
comparison must not demand globally unchanged SQL. A later replacement needs a
real later birth after original retirement, not two simultaneously seeded actors.
Initialized-world/acquisition, the real D owner and held/publication/ACK hooks
remain primary dependencies. Existing helper limitations are not evidence that
those missing native interfaces work.
